### ActivityDataLine

**XSD type:** `ActivityDataLineType`
**Definition:** A class to associate a time period and locations (activity data) with an item for inventory planning purposes.
**DictionaryEntryName:** Activity Data Line. Details
**ObjectClass:** Activity Data Line
